How to use this knowledge base when answering questions about Hypixel SkyBlock Wiki:
- Answer from this content only. If it is not covered, say so; never guess numbers.
- Find relevant pages in index.md by title, aliases (aka) and categories.
- [[X]] links to page X; [[X|y]] and 'y ([[X]])' are text y linking to X. Follow links and each page's 'Linked from' list to gather related facts (e.g. where an item drops or is used).
- Cite the [[Page]] each fact comes from. Reply in the user's language.

# Fortunate Freezing
Fortunate Freezing is a passive event that can happen in the [[Glacite Mineshafts]].
## Mechanics
During the Fortunate Freezing event, players gain 2 [[Mining Fortune]] for every -1 [[Cold]] they have.
## Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Event starting|----------------------------------------------------- FORTUNATE FREEZING STARTED! Passive Active Event Gain +2 ☘ Mining Fortune for every 1 ❄ Cold! -----------------------------------------------------|
## Trivia
- The Fortunate Freezing event has the exact same effect as the Glacial Bonus from the [[Reforging|Glacial]] reforge.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Apr 9, 2024|0.20|Added Fortunate Freezing Event.|
Linked from: [[Cold]], [[Frigid Husk]], [[Glacite Mineshafts]], [[Mining Fortune]]

# Frozen Corpses/Lapis
Lapis Frozen Corpses do not require a key to obtain loot from them.
Upon looting a Lapis Frozen Corpse 3-6 (uniform distribution with an average of 4.5 Rolls) of the below drops will be selected. With [[Heart of the Mountain]] perk Gifts from the Departed at level 100 + [[Drills|Drill]] with [[Blue Cheese Goblin Omelette]] and level 6 [[Grandpa Wolf|Corpse Milestone]], there is a 40.2% chance to gain an additional drop (bringing the average to 4.902).
There is also a 0.0004% (1 in 250,000) chance for each roll to be a [[Frostbitten Dye]] instead, which can be boosted by [[Vincent]] to be 2x or 3x higher.

# Frozen Corpses/Umber and Tungsten
Umber and Tungsten Frozen Corpses share the same loot table. The only functional difference between the two is which key is required to loot them. Umber Frozen Corpses require the use of an [[Umber Key]] to obtain loot from them. Tungsten Frozen Corpses require the use of a [[Tungsten Key]] to obtain loot from them.
Upon looting a Umber or Tungsten Frozen Corpse 4-7 (uniform distribution with an average of 5.5 Rolls) of the below drops will be selected. With [[Heart of the Mountain]] perk Gifts from the Departed at level 100 + [[Drills|Drill]] with [[Blue Cheese Goblin Omelette]] and level 6 [[Grandpa Wolf|Corpse Milestone]], there is a 40.2% chance to gain an additional drop (bringing the average to 5.902).
There is also a 0.001% (1 in 100,000) chance for each roll to be a [[Frostbitten Dye]] instead, which can be boosted by [[Vincent]] to be 2x or 3x higher.

# Frozen Corpses/Vanguard
Vanguard Frozen Corpses require the use of a [[Skeleton Key]] to obtain loot from them and only appear in the Fairy Mineshaft variation of the [[Glacite Mineshafts]].
Upon looting a Vanguard Frozen Corpse 5-8 (uniform distribution with an average of 6.5 Rolls) of the below drops will be selected. With [[Heart of the Mountain]] perk Gifts from the Departed at level 100 + [[Drills|Drill]] with [[Blue Cheese Goblin Omelette]] and level 6 [[Grandpa Wolf|Corpse Milestone]], there is a 40.2% chance to gain an additional drop (bringing the average to 6.902).
There is also a 0.01% (1 in 10,000) chance for each roll to be a [[Frostbitten Dye]] instead, which can be boosted by [[Vincent]] to be 2x or 3x higher.

# Gamemodes
Aliases: Classic, Gamemode
Gamemodes are different modes for [[SkyBlock]] with changes to the game's mechanics. When creating a profile, the player has the option to select a special gamemode. Currently, there are four gamemodes: Classic, [[Stranded|☀ Stranded]], [[Ironman|♲ Ironman]], and [[Bingo|Ⓑ Bingo]].
## Classic
In the Classic (or Normal) gamemode, the player has full access to all features of SkyBlock, including the [[Bazaar]], the [[Auction House]], [[Trading]], and [[Gifts]].
## Ironman
Ironman is the first special gamemode that was introduced to SkyBlock. On an Ironman profile, the [[Auction House]] is disabled, and the Bazaar is restricted to the [[Bazaar|Special Bazaar]]. Ironman players also cannot [[Trading|trade]] with other players (unless in the same co-op) or receive [[Gifts]] from other players.
## Stranded
On a Stranded profile, players cannot leave their [[Private Island]], so there are several changes to make parts of the game's content available. For example, gravel is made when creepers blow up stone.
## Bingo
Bingo profiles are temporary profiles that can be created by talking to the [[Bingo (NPC)|NPC named Bingo]] during a [[Bingo]] event. The event lasts one week, and the profiles are deleted a few days later. In this gamemode, players cannot access the [[Auction House]], use the [[Trading]] system, or spend [[SkyBlock Gems]]. Unlike on Ironman and Stranded, the [[Bazaar]] is completely disabled.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Dec 13, 2021|Added Stranded.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Oct 6, 2020|0.9.6|Added [[Ironman]].|
|Nov 29, 2021|Added [[Bingo]].|
|Jan 18, 2022|0.12.2|Added [[Stranded]].|
Linked from: [[Bingo]], [[Commands]], [[Ironman]], [[Profile]], [[Stranded]], [[Trophy Fish]]

# Gemstone Slot/Format
  Note: The circle ◯ is used to indicate any icon symbol that represents a Slot Type
A locked (and hence unequipped) Gemstone Slot looks like this: [◯] (Icon/Brackets: Dark Gray) An unlocked and unequipped Gemstone Slot looks like this: [◯] (Icon: Gray; Brackets: Dark Gray)
Each equipped Gemstone Slot displays 3 information:
1. The Icon Symbol shows the Gemstone Slot Type (see "Symbol" on the Slot Type table above)
2. The Icon Color shows the type of the applied Gemstone (see table below)
3. The Bracket Color shows the tier of the applied Gemstone (see table below)
Color Representations for the Applied Gemstone:
|Type|Icon Color|Tier|Bracket Color|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ruby Gemstone|Red|Rough|White|
|Amethyst Gemstone|Dark Purple|Flawed|Green|
|Jade Gemstone|Green|Fine|Blue|
|Sapphire Gemstone|Cyan|Flawless|Dark Purple|
|Amber Gemstone|Gold|Perfect|Gold|
|Topaz Gemstone|Yellow|||
|Jasper Gemstone|Light Purple|||
|Opal Gemstone|White|||
|Onyx Gemstone|Dark Gray|||
|Aquamarine Gemstone|Dark Aqua|||
|Citrine Gemstone|Dark Red|||
|Peridot Gemstone|Dark Green|||
Full Listing:

# Harp
Aliases: Melody's Harp
[Harp]
The Harp is a feature located in [[Melody's Plateau]], in front of [[Melody]]. The player can select and play various songs on to earn [[Intelligence]] and [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock XP]].
## Songs
|Song|Difficulty|Reward|Note Sequence|

## Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Harp in Use|[Harp] [player] is already using the Harp!|
|Canceling Song|[Harp] Song canceled!|
|Completing Song|[Harp] Song Completed! [Harp] You scored #% on [song]!|
|[[Intelligence]] Earned|[Harp] Earned +#✎ Intelligence from mastering a new Song!|
## Trivia
- Players with high ping may struggle with the quest and its gameplay, though some methods exist to solve ping and performance issues.
  - By going into Options > Controls and rebinding "Attack/Destroy" and "Pick Block" to the same button, notes will not be picked up when clicked, reducing time and ignoring cooldown.
  - Turning down render distance, closing other windows and turning off unused settings will increase performance.
- The songs Pachelbel and Through The Campfire can only be played on the [[Personal Harp]].
- In total, the rewards are a permanent upgrade of +26 [[Intelligence]], [[SkyBlock Levels|+140 SkyBlock XP]], and the [[Melody's Hair]] accessory.
- If the song being played is canceled, a chicken noise will play, possibly indicating an insult that the player is too chicken to complete the song.
- The song Geothermy? is a reference to the game show Jeopardy!
  - The song title is a play on the title of the show, and the song itself is very similar to the show’s Think Music played during the Final Jeopardy! round.
- The song Godly Imagination is a reference to "Pure Imagination" by Gene Wilder from the 1971 film Willy Wonka & the Chocolate Factory.
- Two [[SkyBlock Levels|Emblems]] are unlocked through the Harp: "Harp Note" by completing all songs and "Harp Master" by perfecting all songs.

# Harvester Helmet Skin/UI

# Hatcessories/Effect
The Hatcessories/Effect grants +1 [[Magic Find]]. It additionally provides +1 [[Intelligence]] for each [[Time Systems|SkyBlock Year]] played when worn. The [[Magic Find]] bonus can only be obtained once from [[Hatcessories]].
When stored in the player's [[Accessory Bag]], the Hatcessories/Effect will count for [[Accessory Power]]. All [[Hatcessories]] count for the same [[Accessory Power]], meaning one of any kind will count for the same as all available ones combined.

# Hoppity's Hunt
Hoppity's Hunt
:
Countdown:
Dates and times based on browser time.
Hoppity's Hunt is an [[Events|Event]] during which players are able to find and collect [[Chocolate Eggs|Chocolate Rabbit Eggs]], as well as shop for [[Chocolate Rabbits]] at [[Hoppity]]. The event takes place once a year starting on Early Spring 1st and lasting just under 3 months (in-game time)
## Stock
Each time [[Hoppity]] arrives, they sell up to 7 random [[Chocolate Rabbits]]. Their selection differs between players and is based on the level of [[Chocolate Factory]] the player has. Upgrading your [[Chocolate Factory]] while the event is active will not change Hoppity's available stock
[[Hoppity]] is also able to sell duplicate [[Chocolate Rabbits]]. Purchasing these will give the player [[Chocolate]] based on the Rabbit's rarity and the player's current Chocolate Per Second.
### Cost
|Rarity|Cost|
|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|250,000 [[Coins]]|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|500,000 [[Coins]]|
|RARE ([[Rarity]])|1,000,000 [[Coins]]|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|2,500,000 [[Coins]]|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|5,000,000 [[Coins]]|
## Chocolate Eggs
While the event is active, [[Chocolate Eggs]] will randomly spawn around the world. They will appear on every island, and are synced meaning it's impossible to get multiple rabbits from one spawn of eggs by going to other islands. They are announced in chat as they appear. Players can search manually or use an [[Egglocator]] to locate them. Once found, they can be clicked to claim a random [[Chocolate Rabbits|Chocolate Rabbit]]. Having a full Rabbit Barn or getting duplicate Rabbits will give the player [[Chocolate]] based on the rarity of the Rabbit and the player's current Chocolate per Second.
|Rarity|Base Amount; (seconds of production)|
|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|150 (2m 30s)|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|300 (5m)|
|RARE ([[Rarity]])|750 (12m 30s)|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|1500|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|4000 (1h 6m 40s)|
|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|8000 (2h 13m 20s)|
|DIVINE ([[Rarity]])|15000 (4h 10m)|
In addition, each duplicate rabbit found gives +10% of the base amount, up to +100% at the 10th duplicate.
The formula is the following, where Duplicates Found is inclusive of the rabbit that you just found. Therefore, your first duplicate will already grant a 10% bonus on top of the base amount.
Chocolate Gained=Chocolate per Second×Base Amount×(1+min⁡(Duplicates Found,10)×0.1)
Only 6 eggs can be active at once. When a new egg appears, the oldest existing one will disappear. Egg locations are consistent between servers. 9 eggs spawn every hour meaning in the event's duration a total of 279 eggs will spawn.
|Chocolate Egg|In-game Spawn Time|Real-world Spawn Time|
|-|-|-|
|Breakfast|7 AM|XX:00:50 XX:20:50 XX:40:50|
|Brunch|7 AM|XX:00:50 XX:20:50 XX:40:50|
|Lunch|2 PM|XX:06:40 XX:26:40 XX:46:40|
|Déjeuner|2 PM|XX:06:40 XX:26:40 XX:46:40|
|Dinner|9 PM|XX:12:30 XX:32:30 XX:52:30|
|Supper|9 PM|XX:12:30 XX:32:30 XX:52:30|
## Tips
- The [[Crystal Hollows]] is the best place to find eggs, as all locations are concentrated in the [[Crystal Nucleus]], making them easily accessible.
  - For players who have not reached [[Heart of the Mountain]] Tier 4, the [[Gold Mine]] is a good alternative.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Apr 9, 2024|0.20|Added Hoppity's Hunt.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Apr 24, 2024|0.20.1|Added Hoppity's Hunt.|
|Apr 28, 2024|First Hoppity's Hunt happens and Chocolate Factory becomes available.|

# Hypixel SkyBlock OST
Aliases: A Silent Memoir, Abstract Ringing, Always Nether, Ambient Caves, Blastin Banter Battle, Blastin banter battle, Blockjitsu, Breathless Encounter, Carnival (soundtrack), Dark and Seedy, Dark and Seedy (Pre-Revolt), Dead Mines, Diggidi Dee, Dungeon Drama, Fires of Fishermen, Forsaken Village, Glacite Tunnels (soundtrack), Going Even Deeper, HSBOST, HSBOSTs, Hallow Hallow, Hypixel OST, Journey in the Sky, Lava Kraken, Let Them Eat Cake, Light From Afar, Magetown, Music, Mythic Warfare, Necron Doom, OST, Rift (soundtrack), ShinkoNet, Silent Wish, Sky of Trees, Superior Judgement, Targets, The Watcher (soundtrack), The Wither King (soundtrack), Tuna Sandwich, Tuna sandwich
[[SkyBlock|Hypixel SkyBlock]] contains lots of in-game music, which are referred to as the Hypixel SkyBlock OST. Tracks in-game are composed by 7[[Ranks|[YOUTUBE]]] ShinkoNet and 5[[Ranks|[MVP+]]] SirCatcelot. Additionally, tracks originating from vanilla Minecraft, composed by C418, appear in-game.
7[[Ranks|[YOUTUBE]]] ShinkoNet was the primary composer for Hypixel SkyBlock between 2019 and 2026, with 5[[Ranks|[MVP+]]] SirCatcelot and Oscar Garvin, the latter being the composer for Hytale, taking over after 2026.

## Trivia
- After the Floating Islands merge (The Park update), the "Sky of Trees" track no longer played.
  - This was fixed in a later update.

[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:

|Jul 10, 2026|Removed "A Silent Memoir".|
[SkyBlock Prototype]

# Interlang
This page is a portal for language projects on Hypixel SkyBlock Wiki. This page is not game content. You are visiting the parent directory of all language projects.
The Language Project for Translators is a project for translation to be done on this wiki. Once a project is big enough with a good amount of editors, a language wiki will be set up for that community. Read more here.
## List of Languages
These are the list of languages that had contributors put in at least one page.
- [[Interlang:Sv|Swedish LP]]

# Interlang:Sv/wiki
Following our migration to Weird Gloop in April 2026, language projects have been deprecated and are no longer supported.
Välkommen till Hypixel SkyBlock Wikin: Encyklopediet för din SkyBlock-resa.
## Hypixel SkyBlock Wikin
Hypixel SkyBlock wikin är ett spel-encyklopedi av Hypixel SkyBlock drivet av gemenskapen. Sedan den 12te Juni 2019 (detta gäller den engelska verisionen, inte den svenska) har Hypixel SkyBlock Wikin skrivits och omarbetats av medlemmar som kommit hit för att redigera den. Wikins syfte är att vara en hubb för information för SkyBlock-spelare, med utvecklande funktioner och innehåll med den bästa precisionen.
Hypixel SkyBlock is a trademark of Hypixel Inc. Hypixel SkyBlock Wiki is in no way affiliated with Hypixel, Mojang, or Microsoft, nor is this wiki monitored by or in contact with Hypixel or Mojang staff. License and Fair Use
## Bläddra mellan spel-funktioner
